> DEVELOPER

let's take a look at the mcp panorama: Currently, the Public Services landscape supports two viewing modes: Panorama and List. I would like to add a third, more intuitive panorama mode that allows users to view the MCP status of all public target systems within a single, windowed viewport. In the current panorama view, when there are too many tools and MCPs, users are forced to use scrollbars to see everything. can you do that?

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import type { McpStatus } from "~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape"
3	import type {
4	  Group,
5	  LayerPayload,
6	  McpDto,
7	  ToolDto,
8	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
9	
10	definePageMeta({ layout: "browse" })
11	
12	const { t } = useI18n()
13	const head = useLocaleHead()
14	useHead(() => ({
15	  title: t("mcpPanorama.page.title"),
16	  htmlAttrs: head.value.htmlAttrs ?? {},
17	}))
18	
19	const {
20	  layer,
21	  primary: activePrimary,
22	  secondary: activeSecondary,
23	  status: statusFilter,
24	  viewMode,
25	  setLayer,
26	  setDrill,
27	  clearDrill,
28	  setStatus,
29	  toggleStatus,
30	  setView,
31	} = usePanoramaState()
32	
33	const active = ref<{ tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto } | null>(null)
34	
35	// Re-fetch when layer changes; everything else filters client-side.
36	const { data, pending, error, refresh } = await useFetch<LayerPayload>(
37	  "/api/internal/mcp-landscape",
38	  {
39	    query: computed(() => ({ layer: layer.value })),
40	    key: "mcp-landscape",
41	  },
42	)
43	
44	// Clear the detail panel when the layer changes; drill state is already
45	// reset inside the composable's setLayer.
46	watch(layer, () => {
47	  active.value = null
48	})
49	
50	// All MCPs for the current layer (used for derived counts).
51	const allMcps = computed<{ tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto }[]>(() => {
52	  if (!data.value) return []
53	  const out: { tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto }[] = []
54	  for (const g of data.value.groups) {
55	    for (const t of g.items) for (const m of t.mcps) out.push({ tool: t, mcp: m })
56	  }
57	  return out
58	})
59	
60	function inDrill(tool: ToolDto): boolean {
61	  if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) return false
62	  if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) return false
63	  return true
64	}
65	
66	function passesStatus(mcp: McpDto): boolean {
67	  return statusFilter.value === "all" || mcp.status === statusFilter.value
68	}
69	
70	// Re-shape groups with filtered MCPs, dropping tools that lose all their MCPs
71	// after the status filter, and dropping empty groups/PDTs.
72	const filteredGroups = computed<Group[]>(() => {
73	  if (!data.value) return []
74	  function filterTools(tools: ToolDto[]): ToolDto[] {
75	    return tools
76	      .filter(inDrill)
77	      .map((tool) => ({
78	        ...tool,
79	        mcps: tool.mcps.filter(passesStatus),
80	      }))
81	      .filter((tool) => tool.mcps.length > 0)
82	  }
83	  return data.value.groups
84	    .map((g): Group => {
85	      const items = filterTools(g.items)
86	      if (g.kind === "sector") {
87	        return { ...g, items, stats: computeStats(items) }
88	      }
89	      const pdts = g.pdts
90	        .map((p) => ({ ...p, items: filterTools(p.items) }))
91	        .filter((p) => p.items.length > 0)
92	      return { ...g, items, pdts, stats: computeStats(items) }
93	    })
94	    .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0)
95	})
96	
97	function computeStats(items: ToolDto[]) {
98	  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0 }
99	  let total = 0
100	  for (const t of items) {
101	    for (const m of t.mcps) {
102	      counts[m.status]++
103	      total++
104	    }
105	  }
106	  if (total === 0) {
107	    return { total, counts, releasedPct: 0, activePct: 0, lagPct: 0 }
108	  }
109	  return {
110	    total,
111	    counts,
112	    releasedPct: Math.round((counts.released / total) * 100),
113	    activePct: Math.round(((counts.released + counts.dev) / total) * 100),
114	    lagPct: Math.round((counts.none / total) * 100),
115	  }
116	}
117	
118	// Visible counts for the section header subtitle and filter chips. These
119	// reflect drill-down but ignore the status filter — chips show how many
120	// MCPs each status would surface if selected.
121	const visibleCounts = computed(() => {
122	  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0, total: 0 }
123	  for (const { tool, mcp } of allMcps.value) {
124	    if (!inDrill(tool)) continue
125	    counts[mcp.status]++
126	    counts.total++
127	  }
128	  return counts
129	})
130	
131	const totals = computed(() => ({ total: data.value?.layerStats.total ?? 0 }))
132	
133	function pickMcp(payload: { tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto }) {
134	  active.value = payload
135	}
136	
137	function drillTo(primary: string) {
138	  setDrill(primary, null)
139	  active.value = null
140	}
141	
142	function filterTo(status: McpStatus) {
143	  // Same toggle behavior as the StatusChip buttons in SectionHeader —
144	  // clicking the active status flips back to "all".
145	  toggleStatus(status)
146	}
147	</script>
148	
149	<template>
150	  <div class="px-6 py-8">
151	    <SectionHeader
152	      v-if="data"
153	      :layer="layer"
154	      :active-primary="activePrimary"
155	      :active-secondary="activeSecondary"
156	      :visible-counts="visibleCounts"
157	      :totals="totals"
158	      :status-filter="statusFilter"
159	      :view-mode="viewMode"
160	      :groups="data.groups"
161	      @update:status-filter="setStatus"
162	      @update:view-mode="setView"
163	      @update:layer="setLayer"
164	      @clear-drill="clearDrill"
165	    />
166	
167	    <div v-if="pending && !data" class="pt-2 text-(--color-ink-muted)">
168	      <div class="h-6 w-40 rounded bg-(--color-border) animate-pulse" />
169	    </div>
170	
171	    <div v-if="error" class="pt-2">
172	      <div class="text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[13px] mb-2">{{ t("mcpPanorama.page.errorLoad") }}</div>
173	      <button
174	        type="button"
175	        class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg) text-[12px] font-medium cursor-pointer"
176	        @click="refresh()"
177	      >
178	        {{ t("mcpPanorama.page.retry") }}
179	      </button>
180	    </div>
181	
182	    <PanoramaView
183	      v-if="data && viewMode === 'panorama'"
184	      :layer="layer"
185	      :stats="!activePrimary && !activeSecondary && statusFilter === 'all'
186	        ? data.layerStats
187	        : computeStats(filteredGroups.flatMap((g) => g.items))"
188	      :groups="filteredGroups"
189	      :active-mcp-id="active?.mcp.id ?? null"
190	      @pick="pickMcp"
191	      @drill="drillTo"
192	      @filter="filterTo"
193	    />
194	    <GroupedListView
195	      v-else-if="data && viewMode === 'list'"
196	      :layer="layer"
197	      :groups="filteredGroups"
198	      :active-mcp-id="active?.mcp.id ?? null"
199	      @pick="pickMcp"
200	    />
201	
202	    <ToolDetailPanel
203	      :active="active"
204	      :groups="data?.groups ?? []"
205	      @close="active = null"
206	      @switch-mcp="pickMcp"
207	    />
208	  </div>
209	</template>
210

```
1	import type { McpStatus } from "~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape"
2	import type { Layer } from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
3	
4	const LAYERS = ["public", "industry"] as const satisfies readonly Layer[]
5	const STATUSES = ["all", "released", "dev", "none"] as const
6	const VIEWS = ["panorama", "list"] as const
7	
8	export type StatusFilter = (typeof STATUSES)[number]
9	export type ViewMode = (typeof VIEWS)[number]
10	
11	function parseEnum<T extends readonly string[]>(
12	  value: unknown,
13	  allowed: T,
14	  fallback: T[number],
15	): T[number] {
16	  if (typeof value !== "string") return fallback
17	  return (allowed as readonly string[]).includes(value) ? (value as T[number]) : fallback
18	}
19	
20	function parseString(value: unknown): string | null {
21	  return typeof value === "string" && value.length > 0 ? value : null
22	}
23	
24	export function usePanoramaState() {
25	  const route = useRoute()
26	  const router = useRouter()
27	
28	  const layer = computed<Layer>(() => parseEnum(route.query.layer, LAYERS, "public"))
29	  const primary = computed<string | null>(() => parseString(route.query.primary))
30	  const secondary = computed<string | null>(() => parseString(route.query.secondary))
31	  const status = computed<StatusFilter>(() => parseEnum(route.query.status, STATUSES, "all"))
32	  const viewMode = computed<ViewMode>(() => parseEnum(route.query.view, VIEWS, "panorama"))
33	
34	  function push(partial: Record<string, string | null>) {
35	    const merged: Record<string, string | null> = {}
36	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
37	      if (typeof v === "string") merged[k] = v
38	    }
39	    Object.assign(merged, partial)
40	    const next: Record<string, string> = {}
41	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(merged)) {
42	      if (v !== null) next[k] = v
43	    }
44	    router.replace({ path: route.path, query: next })
45	  }
46	
47	  function setLayer(value: Layer) {
48	    push({ layer: value === "public" ? null : value, primary: null, secondary: null })
49	  }
50	
51	  function setDrill(primaryKey: string | null, secondaryKey: string | null = null) {
52	    push({ primary: primaryKey, secondary: secondaryKey })
53	  }
54	
55	  function clearDrill() {
56	    push({ primary: null, secondary: null })
57	  }
58	
59	  function setStatus(value: StatusFilter) {
60	    push({ status: value === "all" ? null : value })
61	  }
62	
63	  function toggleStatus(value: McpStatus) {
64	    push({ status: status.value === value ? null : value })
65	  }
66	
67	  function setView(value: ViewMode) {
68	    push({ view: value === "panorama" ? null : value })
69	  }
70	
71	  return {
72	    layer,
73	    primary,
74	    secondary,
75	    status,
76	    viewMode,
77	    setLayer,
78	    setDrill,
79	    clearDrill,
80	    setStatus,
81	    toggleStatus,
82	    setView,
83	  }
84	}
85	
```

11	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
12	import StatusChip from "./StatusChip.vue"

14	const props = defineProps<{

16	  activePrimary: string | null
17	  activeSecondary: string | null
18	  visibleCounts: { total: number; released: number; dev: number; none: number }
19	  totals: { total: number }
20	  statusFilter: "all" | McpStatus
21	  viewMode: "panorama" | "list"
22	  groups: Group[]
23	}>()
24	
25	const emit = defineEmits<{
26	  "update:statusFilter": ["all" | McpStatus]
27	  "update:viewMode": ["panorama" | "list"]
28	  "update:layer": [Layer]

30	}>()
31	
32	const { locale, t } = useI18n()
33	
34	const layerLabel = computed(() => t(`mcpPanorama.layer.${props.layer}`))
35	
36	const titleAndCrumb = computed<{ title: string; crumb: string | null }>(() => {
37	  if (!props.activePrimary) {
38	    return { title: t("mcpPanorama.layer." + props.layer), crumb: null }
39	  }
40	  const primary = props.groups.find((g) => g.key === props.activePrimary)
41	  if (!primary) return { title: props.activePrimary, crumb: layerLabel.value }
42	  if (props.layer === "industry") {
43	    return {
44	      title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value),
45	      crumb: layerLabel.value,
46	    }
47	  }
48	  if (primary.kind !== "domain") {
49	    return { title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value), crumb: layerLabel.value }
50	  }
51	  if (props.activeSecondary) {
52	    const pdt = primary.pdts.find((p) => p.key === props.activeSecondary) as PdtBlock | undefined
53	    if (pdt) {
54	      return {
55	        title: pdtDisplayTitle(pdt, locale.value),
56	        crumb: `${layerLabel.value} / ${groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value)}`,
57	      }
58	    }
59	  }
60	  return {
61	    title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value),
62	    crumb: layerLabel.value,
63	  }
64	})
65	
66	const subtitle = computed(() => {
67	  if (!props.activePrimary) {
68	    return t("mcpPanorama.page.subtitleAll", {
69	      visible: props.visibleCounts.total,
70	      total: props.totals.total,
71	    })
72	  }
73	  return t("mcpPanorama.page.subtitleScoped", {
74	    visible: props.visibleCounts.total,
75	    released: props.visibleCounts.released,
76	    dev: props.visibleCounts.dev,
77	    none: props.visibleCounts.none,
78	  })
79	})
80	
81	function statusLabel(s: McpStatus): string {
82	  return t(`mcpPanorama.status.${s}.label`)
83	}
84	</script>
85	
86	<template>

88	    <!-- Mobile-only layer toggle (sidebar is hidden below md) -->

90	      <button
91	        type="button"
92	        class="flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-[12px] transition-colors"
93	        :class="layer === 'industry'

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import {
4	  mcpDisplayName,
5	  toolDisplayName,
6	  type McpDto,
7	  type ToolDto,
8	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
9	
10	const props = withDefaults(
11	  defineProps<{
12	    tool: ToolDto
13	    mcp: McpDto
14	    active?: boolean
15	    /** Compact = used inside ToolMcpsCard. */
16	    compact?: boolean
17	  }>(),
18	  { active: false, compact: false },
19	)
20	
21	const emit = defineEmits<{ pick: [{ tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto }] }>()
22	
23	const { locale, t } = useI18n()
24	const localePath = useLocalePath()
25	
26	const isReleased = computed(() => props.mcp.status === "released")
27	const showDeps = computed(() => props.mcp.depsCount >= 10)
28	
29	// Placeholder pills are visually quiet — the tool name is already in the
30	// card header above, so the pill just signals "no MCP yet" with an em dash.
31	const displayName = computed(() => {
32	  if (props.mcp.isPlaceholder) return "—"
33	  return mcpDisplayName(props.mcp, locale.value)
34	})
35	
36	const tooltip = computed(() => {
37	  const statusLabel = t(`mcpPanorama.status.${props.mcp.status}.label`)
38	  const toolName = toolDisplayName(props.tool, locale.value)
39	  if (props.mcp.isPlaceholder) {
40	    return `${toolName} · ${statusLabel} (${t("mcpPanorama.detail.notAvailable")})`
41	  }
42	  const mcpName = mcpDisplayName(props.mcp, locale.value)
43	  if (isReleased.value) {
44	    return `${mcpName} · ${toolName} · ${t("mcpPanorama.detail.openInMarketplace")} →`
45	  }
46	  return `${mcpName} · ${toolName} · ${statusLabel} (${t("mcpPanorama.detail.notAvailable")})`
47	})
48	
49	const baseClass = computed(() => [
50	  "group inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full leading-none font-medium tracking-tight no-underline relative shrink-0 transition-all",
51	  props.mcp.status === "released"
52	    && "bg-(--color-status-released-bg) text-(--color-status-released)",
53	  props.mcp.status === "dev"
54	    && "bg-(--color-status-dev-bg) text-(--color-status-dev)",
55	  props.mcp.status === "none"
56	    && "bg-(--color-status-none-bg) text-(--color-status-none)",
57	  props.compact ? "px-2 py-[3px] text-[11px]" : "px-2.5 py-[5px] text-[12px]",
58	  props.active && isReleased.value
59	    && "ring-2 ring-(--color-status-released)/30 ring-offset-1 ring-offset-(--color-card)",
60	  isReleased.value
61	    && "cursor-pointer hover:-translate-y-px hover:bg-(--color-status-released)/15",
62	])
63	
64	function onClick(e: MouseEvent) {
65	  if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.button === 1) return
66	  emit("pick", { tool: props.tool, mcp: props.mcp })
67	}
68	
69	function onStaticPick() {
70	  emit("pick", { tool: props.tool, mcp: props.mcp })
71	}
72	</script>
73	
74	<template>
75	  <NuxtLink
76	    v-if="isReleased && mcp.extensionSlug"
77	    :to="localePath(`/extensions/${mcp.extensionSlug}`)"
78	    :title="tooltip"
79	    :class="baseClass"
80	    @click="onClick"
81	  >
82	    <span class="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">{{ displayName }}</span>
83	    <span
84	      v-if="showDeps"
85	      class="font-mono text-[9px] opacity-70 shrink-0"
86	    >· {{ mcp.depsCount }}</span>
87	    <ChevronRight :size="9" class="shrink-0 opacity-70" aria-hidden="true" />
88	  </NuxtLink>
89	  <button
90	    v-else
91	    type="button"
92	    :title="tooltip"
93	    :class="[...baseClass, 'cursor-pointer']"
94	    @click="onStaticPick"
95	  >
96	    <span class="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">{{ displayName }}</span>
97	    <span
98	      v-if="showDeps"
99	      class="font-mono text-[9px] opacity-70 shrink-0"
100	    >· {{ mcp.depsCount }}</span>
101	  </button>
102	</template>
103

1	// Types and helpers shared between the MCP Panorama API and its UI.
2	
3	import type { McpStatus } from "~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape"
4	
5	export type Layer = "industry" | "public"
6	
7	/**
8	 * A single MCP server. This is the leaf entity rendered as a tile on the
9	 * panorama — a tool may expose 0–N MCPs, plus a synthesized placeholder when
10	 * none exist so the inventory stays visible.
11	 */
12	export interface McpDto {
13	  /** mcp_landscape_mcps.id, or a synthesized negative id when this is a
14	   * placeholder MCP for a tool that doesn't expose any. */
15	  id: number
16	  slug: string
17	  name: string
18	  nameZh: string | null
19	  status: McpStatus
20	  depsCount: number
21	  blurb: string
22	  blurbZh: string
23	  tags: string[]
24	  /** When status === "released", the marketplace listing slug. */
25	  extensionSlug: string | null
26	  /** True when this row was synthesized client/server-side because the tool
27	   * has no MCPs yet; rendered greyed-out without a marketplace link. */
28	  isPlaceholder: boolean
29	}
30	
31	/**
32	 * A PDT software tool — the *product* that owns one or more MCPs. The tile
33	 * row is grouped under its tool header in the panorama.
34	 */
35	export interface ToolDto {
36	  id: number
37	  slug: string
38	  name: string
39	  nameZh: string | null
40	  blurb: string
41	  blurbZh: string
42	  ownerPrimary: string
43	  ownerSecondary: string | null
44	  /** Always ≥ 1 — a placeholder is synthesized when the tool has no real MCPs. */
45	  mcps: McpDto[]
46	  /** "released wins" rollup: released if any MCP is released, else dev if any
47	   * is in-dev, else none. Used for tool-level summaries; tiles render per-MCP. */
48	  rollupStatus: McpStatus
49	}
50	
51	export interface StatusCounts {
52	  released: number
53	  dev: number
54	  none: number
55	}
56	
57	/** Counts are over **MCPs** (the leaf tile entity), not tools. */
58	export interface GroupStats {
59	  total: number
60	  counts: StatusCounts
61	  releasedPct: number
62	  activePct: number
63	  lagPct: number
64	}
65	
66	export interface PdtBlock {
67	  key: string
68	  label: string
69	  labelZh: string
70	  items: ToolDto[]
71	}
72	
73	export interface SectorGroup {
74	  kind: "sector"
75	  key: string
76	  label: string
77	  labelZh: string
78	  short: string
79	  items: ToolDto[]
80	  stats: GroupStats
81	}
82	
83	export interface DomainGroup {
84	  kind: "domain"
85	  key: string
86	  label: string
87	  labelZh: string
88	  short: string
89	  items: ToolDto[]
90	  pdts: PdtBlock[]
91	  stats: GroupStats
92	}
93	
94	export type Group = SectorGroup | DomainGroup
95	
96	export interface LayerPayload {
97	  layer: Layer
98	  layerStats: GroupStats
99	  groups: Group[]
100	}
101	
102	export type RankKey = "leading" | "onTrack" | "lagging" | "early"
103	
104	/** Returns the rank label for a group's stats — null if too small or middling. */
105	export function rankFor(stats: GroupStats): RankKey | null {
106	  if (stats.total < 3) return null
107	  if (stats.releasedPct >= 75) return "leading"
108	  if (stats.releasedPct >= 50) return "onTrack"
109	  if (stats.lagPct >= 50) return "lagging"
110	  if (stats.releasedPct < 25) return "early"
111	  return null
112	}
113	
114	export const STATUS_ORDER: McpStatus[] = ["none", "dev", "released"]
115	
116	/** Localised display name for a tool (Chinese fallback to English when null). */
117	export function toolDisplayName(tool: ToolDto, locale: string): string {
118	  if (locale === "zh" && tool.nameZh) return tool.nameZh
119	  return tool.name
120	}
121	
122	/** Localised display blurb for a tool. */
123	export function toolDisplayBlurb(tool: ToolDto, locale: string): string {
124	  return locale === "zh" ? tool.blurbZh : tool.blurb
125	}
126	
127	/** Localised display name for an MCP (Chinese fallback to English). */
128	export function mcpDisplayName(mcp: McpDto, locale: string): string {
129	  if (locale === "zh" && mcp.nameZh) return mcp.nameZh
130	  return mcp.name
131	}
132	
133	/** Localised display blurb for an MCP. */
134	export function mcpDisplayBlurb(mcp: McpDto, locale: string): string {
135	  return locale === "zh" ? mcp.blurbZh : mcp.blurb
136	}
137	
138	/** Localised group title. */
139	export function groupDisplayTitle(g: Group, locale: string): string {
140	  return locale === "zh" ? g.labelZh : g.label
141	}
142	
143	export function pdtDisplayTitle(p: PdtBlock, locale: string): string {
144	  return locale === "zh" ? p.labelZh : p.label
145	}
146

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import {
3	  groupDisplayTitle,
4	  pdtDisplayTitle,
5	  STATUS_ORDER,
6	  type Group,
7	  type Layer,
8	  type McpDto,
9	  type ToolDto,
10	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
11	import ToolCard from "./ToolCard.vue"
12	
13	const props = defineProps<{
14	  layer: Layer
15	  groups: Group[]
16	  activeMcpId: number | null
17	}>()
18	const emit = defineEmits<{ pick: [{ tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto }] }>()
19	
20	const { locale, t } = useI18n()
21	
22	interface ToolMcpPair {
23	  tool: ToolDto
24	  mcp: McpDto
25	}
26	
27	interface FlatGroup {
28	  key: string
29	  title: string
30	  subtitle?: string
31	  items: ToolDto[]
32	  pairs: ToolMcpPair[]
33	}
34	
35	function flatten(items: ToolDto[]): ToolMcpPair[] {
36	  const out: ToolMcpPair[] = []
37	  for (const tool of items) for (const mcp of tool.mcps) out.push({ tool, mcp })
38	  return out
39	}
40	
41	const flatGroups = computed<FlatGroup[]>(() => {
42	  if (props.layer === "industry") {
43	    return props.groups.map((g) => ({
44	      key: g.key,
45	      title: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),
46	      items: g.items,
47	      pairs: flatten(g.items),
48	    }))
49	  }
50	  const out: FlatGroup[] = []
51	  for (const g of props.groups) {
52	    if (g.kind !== "domain") continue
53	    for (const p of g.pdts) {
54	      out.push({
55	        key: `${g.key}.${p.key}`,
56	        title: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),
57	        subtitle: pdtDisplayTitle(p, locale.value),
58	        items: p.items,
59	        pairs: flatten(p.items),
60	      })
61	    }
62	  }
63	  return out
64	})
65	
66	function pairsByStatus(pairs: ToolMcpPair[], status: typeof STATUS_ORDER[number]) {
67	  return pairs.filter((p) => p.mcp.status === status)
68	}
69	</script>
70	
71	<template>
72	  <div class="px-7 pb-7 flex flex-col gap-5">
73	    <section v-for="g in flatGroups" :key="g.key">
74	      <div class="flex items-baseline justify-between px-1 pb-2.5">
75	        <div class="flex items-baseline gap-2.5">
76	          <h3 class="font-serif text-[20px] font-medium tracking-tight m-0 text-(--color-ink)">
77	            {{ g.title }}
78	          </h3>
79	          <span v-if="g.subtitle" class="font-mono text-[13px] text-(--color-ink-muted)">
80	            / {{ g.subtitle }}
81	          </span>
82	        </div>

> AGENT

I've read through the panorama implementation — there are currently two view modes (`panorama` and `list`) wired through `usePanoramaState`, with the toggle in `SectionHeader.vue` and rendering in `PanoramaView.vue` / `GroupedListView.vue`. Adding a third mode is structurally straightforward (extend the `VIEWS` tuple, add a toggle button, render a new component), but the visual design of the "single-viewport" mode is a meaningful product decision and there are a few different ways to interpret "fit everything in one viewport." Let me confirm the direction before I build it.

> TOOL

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport {\n  groupDisplayTitle,\n  pdtDisplayTitle,\n  STATUS_ORDER,\n  toolDisplayName,\n  type Group,\n  type GroupStats,\n  type Layer,\n  type McpDto,\n  type StatusCounts,\n  type ToolDto,\n} from \"~~/shared/mcp-panorama\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  layer: Layer\n  groups: Group[]\n  activeMcpId: number | null\n}>()\nconst emit = defineEmits<{\n  pick: [{ tool: ToolDto; mcp: McpDto }]\n  drill: [string]\n}>()\n\nconst { locale, t } = useI18n()\n\ninterface OverviewCard {\n  /** Unique key per card — sector key for industry, `${domain}.${pdt}` for public. */\n  key: string\n  /** Drill target — the primary group key (sector or domain). */\n  drillKey: string\n  title: string\n  /** Only set on public layer when the card represents a PDT under a domain. */\n  subtitle: string | null\n  items: ToolDto[]\n  stats: GroupStats\n}\n\nfunction statsFor(items: ToolDto[]): GroupStats {\n  const counts: StatusCounts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0 }\n  let total = 0\n  for (const tool of items) {\n    for (const mcp of tool.mcps) {\n      counts[mcp.status]++\n      total++\n    }\n  }\n  if (total === 0) {\n    return { total, counts, releasedPct: 0, activePct: 0, lagPct: 0 }\n  }\n  return {\n    total,\n    counts,\n    releasedPct: Math.round((counts.released / total) * 100),\n    activePct: Math.round(((counts.released + counts.dev) / total) * 100),\n    lagPct: Math.round((counts.none / total) * 100),\n  }\n}\n\nconst cards = computed<OverviewCard[]>(() => {\n  if (props.layer === \"industry\") {\n    return props.groups.map((g) => ({\n      key: g.key,\n      drillKey: g.key,\n      title: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),\n      subtitle: null,\n      items: g.items,\n      stats: g.stats,\n    }))\n  }\n  const out: OverviewCard[] = []\n  for (const g of props.groups) {\n    if (g.kind !== \"domain\") {\n      out.push({\n        key: g.key,\n        drillKey: g.key,\n        title: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),\n        subtitle: null,\n        items: g.items,\n        stats: g.stats,\n      })\n      continue\n    }\n    for (const pdt of g.pdts) {\n      out.push({\n        key: `${g.key}.${pdt.key}`,\n        drillKey: g.key,\n        title: pdtDisplayTitle(pdt, locale.value),\n        subtitle: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),\n        items: pdt.items,\n        stats: statsFor(pdt.items),\n      })\n    }\n  }\n  return out\n})\n\nfunction dotClass(status: McpDto[\"status\"]): string {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"released\":\n      return \"bg-(--color-status-released)\"\n    case \"dev\":\n      return \"bg-(--color-status-dev)\"\n    default:\n      return \"bg-(--color-status-none)\"\n  }\n}\n\nfunction mcpTitle(tool: ToolDto, mcp: McpDto): string {\n  const toolName = toolDisplayName(tool, locale.value)\n  const statusLabel = t(`mcpPanorama.status.${mcp.status}.short`)\n  if (mcp.isPlaceholder) {\n    return `${toolName} · ${statusLabel}`\n  }\n  const mcpName = locale.value === \"zh\" && mcp.nameZh ? mcp.nameZh : mcp.name\n  return `${toolName} · ${mcpName} · ${statusLabel}`\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-7 pb-7\">\n    <div\n      class=\"grid gap-3 items-stretch\"\n      style=\"grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr)); grid-auto-rows: minmax(0, 1fr);\"\n    >\n      <article\n        v-for=\"card in cards\"\n        :key=\"card.key\"\n        class=\"bg-(--color-card) border border-(--color-border) rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col gap-2 min-w-0 min-h-0 overflow-hidden\"\n      >\n        <header class=\"flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 min-w-0\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            class=\"flex items-baseline gap-1.5 min-w-0 bg-transparent border-0 p-0 cursor-pointer text-left text-(--color-ink) hover:underline underline-offset-4 decoration-(--color-ink-muted) transition-colors\"\n            :title=\"t('mcpPanorama.card.drillIn', { name: card.title })\"\n            @click=\"emit('drill', card.drillKey)\"\n          >\n            <span\n              v-if=\"card.subtitle\"\n              class=\"font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-(--color-ink-muted) shrink-0\"\n            >{{ card.subtitle }} /</span>\n            <span class=\"font-serif text-[14px] font-medium tracking-tight truncate\">\n              {{ card.title }}\n            </span>\n            <span class=\"font-mono text-[10px] text-(--color-ink-muted) shrink-0 tabular-nums\">\n              {{ card.stats.total }}\n            </span>\n          </button>\n          <span class=\"font-mono text-[10px] text-(--color-ink-muted) shrink-0 tabular-nums\">\n            <span class=\"text-(--color-ink)\">{{ card.stats.releasedPct }}%</span>\n            <span class=\"opacity-70 ml-0.5\">{{ t(\"mcpPanorama.card.releasedShort\") }}</span>\n          </span>\n        </header>\n\n        <div class=\"flex h-1 w-full rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-(--color-border) shrink-0\">\n          <template v-for=\"s in STATUS_ORDER\" :key=\"s\">\n            <div\n              v-if=\"card.stats.counts[s] > 0\"\n              :class=\"dotClass(s)\"\n              :style=\"{ width: `${(card.stats.counts[s] / card.stats.total) * 100}%` }\"\n            />\n          </template>\n        </div>\n\n        <ul class=\"flex flex-col gap-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden flex-1\" role=\"list\">\n          <li\n            v-for=\"tool in card.items\"\n            :key=\"tool.id\"\n            class=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0\"\n          >\n            <span\n              class=\"font-serif text-[12px] text-(--color-ink) truncate shrink\"\n              :title=\"toolDisplayName(tool, locale)\"\n            >{{ toolDisplayName(tool, locale) }}</span>\n            <div class=\"flex items-center gap-[3px] flex-wrap justify-end ml-auto\">\n              <button\n                v-for=\"mcp in tool.mcps\"\n                :key=\"mcp.id\"\n                type=\"button\"\n                class=\"size-[7px] rounded-full cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-[1.6]\"\n                :class=\"[\n                  dotClass(mcp.status),\n                  mcp.isPlaceholder && 'opacity-40',\n                  activeMcpId === mcp.id && 'ring-2 ring-(--color-ink)/40 ring-offset-1 ring-offset-(--color-card) scale-[1.4]',\n                ]\"\n                :title=\"mcpTitle(tool, mcp)\"\n                :aria-label=\"mcpTitle(tool, mcp)\"\n                @click=\"emit('pick', { tool, mcp })\"\n              />\n            </div>\n          </li>\n        </ul>\n      </article>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Here's a summary:

## What changed

**New default view "Overview"** — compact group cards with a per-group header (title · count · `% released`), a thin segmented stat bar, and one row per tool showing the tool name + a dot per MCP (color = status, click = open detail panel, drill via header click). Each card uses `min-h-0 / overflow-hidden`, and the grid uses `grid-auto-rows: minmax(0, 1fr)` so groups stretch evenly across the viewport, giving the "single windowed viewport" feel without forcing literal truncation.

**Wiring:**
- `app/composables/usePanoramaState.ts` — added `"overview"` to `VIEWS`, flipped the default from `panorama` → `overview`, and updated `setView` so the default omits the `view=` query param.
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` — new component. On the `industry` layer it renders one card per sector; on `public` it flattens to one card per PDT (with the parent domain as a small mono crumb above the title).
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` — third toggle button with `LayoutDashboard` icon, placed first since it's the new default. Emit type widened to `'overview' | 'panorama' | 'list'`.
- `app/pages/mcp/panorama.vue` — added the `v-if="viewMode === 'overview'"` branch wired to the existing `pickMcp` / `drillTo` handlers.
- `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json` — added `mcpPanorama.view.overview` ("Overview" / "总览").

**Validation:** `lint` clean, `typecheck` exit 0, `vitest` 256/256 passing.

**Worth flagging:** I have not opened a browser to visually confirm density on real data — the project uses `bun nuxt dev` and I can't drive a UI from here. Per CLAUDE.md "for UI changes, start the dev server and use the feature in a browser" — please spot-check both layers (`/mcp/panorama` and `?layer=industry`) and let me know if dot-size or column min-width need tuning. Common likely tweaks: bumping `minmax(260px, 1fr)` up if cards feel cramped on wide screens, or shrinking the dots (currently `size-[7px]`) if a tool with 8+ MCPs wraps awkwardly.

Branch `feat/panorama-overview-mode` is ready; nothing pushed.

Perfect. I have what I need. Here's your structural map:

---

**1. PdtBlock.vue** — Minimal PDT sub-block component (lines 1-42). Props: `pdt`, `activeMcpId`. Layout: header (title + mono mcpCount) + flex-col gap-1.5 of `ToolMcpsCard` rows. **Highly reusable for your nested overview**; it's compact already (px-2.5 py-2.5, font-[13px] header). No visual density issues for an overview context.

**2. DomainCard.vue** — Confirmed: grid `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr))` with `gap-2.5` nesting PDTs. Top-level article is `p-3.5`, wraps `CardHeader` + grid of `PdtBlock`s. **This is your pattern**: you can mirror the domain→PDT nesting at smaller scale for OverviewView's domain-cards-containing-pdt-blocks.

**3. CardHeader.vue** — NOT reusable for inner PDT headers. It's tied to top-level Groups (renders `group.stats.total` and `X/Y released` suffix in monospace). PdtBlock.vue instead builds its own mini header (serif title + mono mcpCount). For your inner PDT block headers in OverviewView, you'd need a smaller variant or follow PdtBlock's pattern directly.

**4. OverviewView.vue** — Current `cards` shape (lines 27–37): flat array with `key`, `drillKey`, `title`, `subtitle` (null on industry, domain crumb on public), `items` (ToolDto[]), `stats`. Per-card render (lines 126–193): single article per card with header + status bar + tool rows with 7px dot MCPs. **Flattened structure; your refactor will nest domain cards containing PDT sub-cards.**

**5. Other components in mcp-landscape/:**
- **SectorCard.vue** — Industry-layer card: CardHeader + `ToolMcpsCard` list in a single rounded div (no nested PDT level). Pattern similar to domain but flat.
- **PanoramaView.vue** — Orchestrator; switches DomainCard vs SectorCard based on layer.
- **ToolMcpsCard.vue**, **ToolDetailPanel.vue** — Tool/MCP detail renderers (not hierarchy-related).
- **LayerSummary.vue**, **GroupedListView.vue**, **StatusChip.vue**, **StatusPill.vue**, **McpTile.vue**, **SectionHeader.vue**, **PanoramaSidebarSection.vue** — Sidebar, summary, and utility components.

**No other hierarchy component exists.** Your refactor plan: replace OverviewView's flat card grid with a domain-card wrapper (using DomainCard's outer styling + gap-2.5 grid for PDTs), then nest PdtBlock inside each domain (or a compact PDT variant), rendering tool rows inside. SectorCard can stay flat on industry layer.

id: toolu_01PJhHJgfdKGh6v29P8oBMoM
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/the-blocks-still-need-fluffy-whistle.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Make Overview blocks show domain → PDT → tool containment\n\n## Context\n\nThe previous turn added a new \"Overview\" view mode to the MCP panorama page (`app/pages/mcp/panorama.vue`) — compact group cards meant to fit everything in one viewport. On the `industry` layer this is a flat sector → tools structure and works fine. On the `public` layer, however, I flattened the domain → PDT → tools hierarchy into one card per PDT (with the parent domain shown only as a small mono crumb above the title). The user flagged that this loses the *containment* signal: a viewer can no longer see at a glance which PDTs belong to which domain.\n\nThis change refines the public-layer rendering so the blocks visually express containment:\n- One outer card per **domain**, with the domain title + rolled-up stats in its header.\n- Inside each domain card, a grid of **PDT sub-blocks**, each with its own mini header (PDT title + MCP count).\n- Inside each PDT sub-block, the compact tool rows (tool name + a colored dot per MCP) already used today.\n\nIndustry layer is unchanged: one card per sector containing tool rows directly (sectors have no PDT level).\n\nThe overview mode stays the default. The \"fit a single viewport\" intent is best-effort: on public layer with many domains × many PDTs, vertical page scroll is acceptable when containment can't be shown otherwise — the user explicitly asked for the hierarchy to be visible, which is incompatible with strict clipping on dense datasets.\n\n## Changes\n\n### `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (rewrite the render half)\n\n**Data shape:** drop the flat `OverviewCard[]` computed. Iterate `props.groups` directly and branch on `g.kind`:\n- `kind === 'sector'` → render `SectorOverviewBlock` (current per-card layout — keep as-is)\n- `kind === 'domain'` → render `DomainOverviewBlock` containing a nested grid of `PdtOverviewSubBlock`s\n\nThis reuses the existing `Group | SectorGroup | DomainGroup | PdtBlock` types from `~~/shared/mcp-panorama` and the already-imported helpers (`groupDisplayTitle`, `pdtDisplayTitle`, `toolDisplayName`). No new shared types.\n\n**Inline PDT stats:** the existing local `statsFor(items: ToolDto[])` helper already computes `GroupStats` for a tool list — keep it and call it per PDT for the inner % bar / count, just as the previous flat layout did.\n\n**Three internal sub-templates (kept in the single SFC — no new files):**\n\n1. **Sector block** (industry) — unchanged from current OverviewView. Header (title · total · `% released`) + thin segmented bar + tool rows (name + dots).\n\n2. **Domain block** (public, outer card) — wraps:\n   - Header: domain title · total · `% released` (clickable → `emit('drill', domain.key)`)\n   - Thin segmented status bar over the *rolled-up* domain stats (already in `g.stats`)\n   - A nested grid `style=\"grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr)); gap: 0.5rem\"` of PDT sub-blocks\n\n3. **PDT sub-block** (public, inner) — minimal nested card:\n   - Tiny header: PDT title (serif, ~12px) + mono total count. No % bar inside the PDT to keep visual noise low; the domain-level bar already conveys progress.\n   - Tool rows (same compact `name + dots` pattern used today)\n   - Subtle styling: `bg-(--color-bg)` (one shade darker than the outer `--color-card`) and a `rounded-md` border, mirroring how `SectorCard.vue` already nests its tool list inside a darker `bg-(--color-bg)` panel — gives the nesting an obvious visual depth without inventing new tokens.\n\n**Outer grid sizing:** because domain cards now contain a nested grid, bump the outer minmax from `260px` to `320px` (`grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))`) so each domain card has room for at least 1–2 PDT sub-columns. Keep `grid-auto-rows: minmax(0, 1fr)` so cards still stretch evenly. Sector cards on industry layer keep the existing `260px` min — sectors are smaller and benefit from more columns.\n\n**Drill behavior:** unchanged from current panorama — emit `drill` with the primary key (sector or domain). ## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Make Overview blocks show domain → PDT → tool containment

## Context

The previous turn added a new "Overview" view mode to the MCP panorama page (`app/pages/mcp/panorama.vue`) — compact group cards meant to fit everything in one viewport. On the `industry` layer this is a flat sector → tools structure and works fine. On the `public` layer, however, I flattened the domain → PDT → tools hierarchy into one card per PDT (with the parent domain shown only as a small mono crumb above the title). The user flagged that this loses the *containment* signal: a viewer can no longer see at a glance which PDTs belong to which domain.

This change refines the public-layer rendering so the blocks visually express containment:
- One outer card per **domain**, with the domain title + rolled-up stats in its header.
- Inside each domain card, a grid of **PDT sub-blocks**, each with its own mini header (PDT title + MCP count).
- Inside each PDT sub-block, the compact tool rows (tool name + a colored dot per MCP) already used today.

Industry layer is unchanged: one card per sector containing tool rows directly (sectors have no PDT level).

The overview mode stays the default. The "fit a single viewport" intent is best-effort: on public layer with many domains × many PDTs, vertical page scroll is acceptable when containment can't be shown otherwise — the user explicitly asked for the hierarchy to be visible, which is incompatible with strict clipping on dense datasets.

## Changes

### `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (rewrite the render half)

**Data shape:** drop the flat `OverviewCard[]` computed. Iterate `props.groups` directly and branch on `g.kind`:
- `kind === 'sector'` → render `SectorOverviewBlock` (current per-card layout — keep as-is)
- `kind === 'domain'` → render `DomainOverviewBlock` containing a nested grid of `PdtOverviewSubBlock`s

This reuses the existing `Group | SectorGroup | DomainGroup | PdtBlock` types from `~~/shared/mcp-panorama` and the already-imported helpers (`groupDisplayTitle`, `pdtDisplayTitle`, `toolDisplayName`). No new shared types.

**Inline PDT stats:** the existing local `statsFor(items: ToolDto[])` helper already computes `GroupStats` for a tool list — keep it and call it per PDT for the inner % bar / count, just as the previous flat layout did.

**Three internal sub-templates (kept in the single SFC — no new files):**

1. **Sector block** (industry) — unchanged from current OverviewView. Header (title · total · `% released`) + thin segmented bar + tool rows (name + dots).

2. **Domain block** (public, outer card) — wraps:
   - Header: domain title · total · `% released` (clickable → `emit('drill', domain.key)`)
   - Thin segmented status bar over the *rolled-up* domain stats (already in `g.stats`)
   - A nested grid `style="grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr)); gap: 0.5rem"` of PDT sub-blocks

3. **PDT sub-block** (public, inner) — minimal nested card:
   - Tiny header: PDT title (serif, ~12px) + mono total count. No % bar inside the PDT to keep visual noise low; the domain-level bar already conveys progress.
   - Tool rows (same compact `name + dots` pattern used today)
   - Subtle styling: `bg-(--color-bg)` (one shade darker than the outer `--color-card`) and a `rounded-md` border, mirroring how `SectorCard.vue` already nests its tool list inside a darker `bg-(--color-bg)` panel — gives the nesting an obvious visual depth without inventing new tokens.

**Outer grid sizing:** because domain cards now contain a nested grid, bump the outer minmax from `260px` to `320px` (`grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))`) so each domain card has room for at least 1–2 PDT sub-columns. Keep `grid-auto-rows: minmax(0, 1fr)` so cards still stretch evenly. Sector cards on industry layer keep the existing `260px` min — sectors are smaller and benefit from more columns.

**Drill behavior:** unchanged from current panorama — emit `drill` with the primary key (sector or domain). The existing panorama doesn't support drilling directly into a specific PDT either; adding that is out of scope for this refactor and can be a follow-up if the user wants it.

### No other files change

- `app/composables/usePanoramaState.ts` — no change, the `overview` mode and its default status from the prior turn stand.
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` — no change, the three-button toggle from the prior turn is correct.
- `app/pages/mcp/panorama.vue` — no change, the `v-if="viewMode === 'overview'"` branch already passes the right props (`groups`, `activeMcpId`, `pick`, `drill`).
- i18n — no new strings needed; existing `mcpPanorama.card.releasedShort`, `mcpPanorama.card.drillIn`, `mcpPanorama.status.*.short` cover everything.

## Verification

1. `bun run lint` — must stay clean.
2. `bun run typecheck` — must exit 0 (same baseline tooling warnings as last run).
3. `bun run test` — all 256 existing tests must keep passing; no new tests required for this purely visual refinement (the underlying `Group` discriminator branching is already covered by `shared/mcp-panorama.test.ts`).
4. **Manual browser check (user-side):** `bun run dev`, then visit:
   - `/en/mcp/panorama` (default = overview, layer = public) — confirm each domain renders as a single outer card containing labeled PDT sub-blocks, each of which contains its tool rows. The domain title + % bar should sit above the PDT grid, not inside it.
   - `/en/mcp/panorama?layer=industry` — confirm sector cards stay flat (no nested PDT level appears, since industry has no PDTs).
   - `/en/mcp/panorama?view=panorama` and `?view=list` — confirm the other two modes are untouched.
   - Click a dot in any PDT sub-block → confirm the detail panel still opens with the right MCP.
   - Click a domain title → confirm it still drills the domain (URL gets `?primary=...`).

## Critical files

- `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (modified — full re-render of template; script gets minor cleanup of the no-longer-needed `cards` flattening for public)
- Reference only (no edits): `app/components/mcp-landscape/DomainCard.vue` (outer pattern), `app/components/mcp-landscape/PdtBlock.vue` (inner pattern), `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectorCard.vue` (nested `bg-(--color-bg)` panel idea), `shared/mcp-panorama.ts` (`Group` discriminator, helpers)

```

> AGENT

Done. Branch `feat/panorama-overview-mode` (untracked `OverviewView.vue` + 5 modified files from the prior turn).

## What changed in this refinement

`app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` was rewritten so the public layer visibly reflects **domain → PDT → tools** containment instead of flattening PDTs to peers:

- **Script** dropped the flat `OverviewCard` shape and the `statsFor` helper. The template now iterates `props.groups` directly and branches on the `Group.kind` discriminator (`sector` vs `domain`), so it reuses the existing `Group | SectorGroup | DomainGroup | PdtBlock` types from `shared/mcp-panorama.ts` without inventing new ones.
- **Sector card** (industry layer) keeps the previous flat layout: title + count + `% released`, segmented status bar, then compact tool rows (name + 7px dots).
- **Domain card** (public layer) keeps the same header + rolled-up status bar, but its body is now a nested grid `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))` of PDT sub-blocks.
- **PDT sub-block** mirrors the existing `PdtBlock.vue` styling — `bg-(--color-bg) rounded-md` for the depth cue — with a tighter mini header (12px serif title + mono count, no % bar to keep noise low) and even more compact tool rows (11px name + 6px dots) so the inner cells stay legible.
- Outer grid min column width bumped to `320px` on public layer (sectors stay at `260px`) so domain cards have room for at least 1–2 PDT sub-columns.
- Active-MCP ring uses `ring-offset-(--color-bg)` inside PDT sub-blocks (the darker panel) and the original `--color-card` in sector rows, so the focus halo reads correctly against either background.

Drill behavior unchanged from the prior turn and matches the regular panorama: domain title click drills the primary; PDT title click is non-interactive (consistent with how `PdtBlock.vue` works today). Tool/MCP `pick` events still flow up unchanged.

## Verification

- `bun run lint` — clean.
- `bun run typecheck` — exit 0 (same pre-existing tooling warnings).
- `bun run test` — 256/256 pass, 28 files.
- **Not done by me:** browser sanity check on `/en/mcp/panorama` (public, default), `?layer=industry`, `?view=panorama`, and `?view=list`. Per the plan's verification step, please spot-check density on real data — likely tuning candidates if anything feels off are the inner PDT minmax (currently `150px`) and dot size (`6px` inside PDTs, `7px` inside sectors).

1	# Plan: Make Overview blocks show domain → PDT → tool containment
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	The previous turn added a new "Overview" view mode to the MCP panorama page (`app/pages/mcp/panorama.vue`) — compact group cards meant to fit everything in one viewport. On the `industry` layer this is a flat sector → tools structure and works fine. On the `public` layer, however, I flattened the domain → PDT → tools hierarchy into one card per PDT (with the parent domain shown only as a small mono crumb above the title). The user flagged that this loses the *containment* signal: a viewer can no longer see at a glance which PDTs belong to which domain.
6	
7	This change refines the public-layer rendering so the blocks visually express containment:
8	- One outer card per **domain**, with the domain title + rolled-up stats in its header.
9	- Inside each domain card, a grid of **PDT sub-blocks**, each with its own mini header (PDT title + MCP count).

11

13	
14	The overview mode stays the default. The "fit a single viewport" intent is best-effort: on public layer with many domains × many PDTs, vertical page scroll is acceptable when containment can't be shown otherwise — the user explicitly asked for the hierarchy to be visible, which is incompatible with strict clipping on dense datasets.

Perfect. Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile my findings:

## Structural Ground Truth Report: MCP Landscape Super-Bucketing

**Scope:** Database schema, seed data, server query layer, client state, OverviewView markup.

---

### 1. Database / Shared Schema

**Tables present:**
- `mcp_sectors` (12 records) — industry layer grouping
- `mcp_domains` (5 records) — public layer grouping
- `mcp_pdts` (multiple records) — sub-grouping within domains
- `mcp_landscape_tools` — products, owned by sector or domain+PDT
- `mcp_landscape_mcps` — leaf MCP servers per tool

**Super-domain column?** None exists. The schema has a **binary layer enum** (`industry | public`) at the tool level (`mcp_landscape_tools.layer`), but **no intermediate hierarchy above domains**.

**Layer storage:** Computed at query time via `mcpLandscapeTools.layer` (DB column) and `Layer` type (`"industry" | "public"` in TypeScript). The layer is already in the DB; it's **not** computed at render time from the owner path.

---

### 2. Seed Data Inventory

**Existing domains (public layer):**
1. `airnd` — "AI R&D" ✓
2. `prodsw` — "Product & Software" ✓
3. `hardware` — "Hardware"
4. `proddigi` — "Product Digitization"
5. `infra` — "Infrastructure"

**Existing sectors (industry layer):** 12 sectors (wireless, datacom, cloud, terminals, optical, carrier, enterprise, consumer, energy, auto, smartcity, industrial).

**Judgment:** The 5 public domains map **almost** cleanly to your 3-bucket proposal:
- **AI R&D** (airnd) → "AI R&D" bucket ✓ (exact match)
- **Product & Software** (prodsw) → "Product and Software" bucket ✓ (exact match)
- Hardware + Product Digitization + Infrastructure → "Other domains" bucket (consolidation needed)

---

### 3. Server Query Layer

**`getLandscape(layer: Layer)`** assembly (lines 95–240):
1. Fetches tools by layer from DB (via `where(eq(layer, layer))`)
2. Fetches all MCPs for those tools
3. **For industry:** groups by sector (primary)
4. **For public:** groups by domain (primary) + nested PDTs (secondary)
5. Returns `LayerPayload { layer, layerStats, groups: Group[] }`

**Where does layer come from?** Always from `mcpLandscapeTools.layer` DB column, **never computed**. The grouping logic is purely database-driven; there's no runtime transform to a "super-layer" concept.

**Could super-grouping be API-side?** Yes, but would require adding a new field to the `Group` union type (both `SectorGroup` and `DomainGroup`) to carry a `superBucket?: string`. Alternatively, the client could transform the response — lighter touch.

---

### 4. Layer Toggle Wiring

**`usePanoramaState.ts`** (lines 4–6, 28, 47–49):
- Binary toggle: `layer: "public" | "industry"`
- No mention of "AI R&D", "Product and Software" anywhere in the composable
- `setLayer(value)` resets drill state (`primary`, `secondary` nulled)
- **Sidebar reference**: Confirmed in `Sidebar.vue` that `isPanoramaRoute` watches the `/mcp/panorama` route; no special layer-switching logic beyond the binary toggle

**No hint of 3-bucket concept anywhere in the codebase** — neither as a named "view dimension" nor as a hardcoded mapping.

---

### 5. OverviewView Current Markup

**Tool row** (lines 108–133, sector/domain versions share same pattern):
```vue
<li class="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
  <span class="font-serif text-[12px] text-(--color-ink) truncate shrink">
    {{ toolDisplayName(tool, locale) }}
  </span>
  <div class="flex items-center gap-[3px] flex-wrap justify-end ml-auto">
    <button v-for="mcp in tool.mcps" type="button"
      class="size-[7px] rounded-full cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-[1.6]"
      :class="[dotClass(mcp.status), ...]"
      @click="emit('pick', { tool, mcp })"
    />
  </div>
</li>
```
**Status:** 7px circular dots (sector), 6px (PDT sub-blocks), rendered **inline after tool name**, no "status-after-name" label suffix yet.

**Status bar** (lines 91–100, domain card level):
```vue
<div class="flex h-1 w-full rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-(--color-border) shrink-0">
  <template v-for="s in STATUS_ORDER">
    <div v-if="g.stats.counts[s] > 0"
      :class="dotClass(s)"
      :style="{ width: `${(g.stats.counts[s] / g.stats.total) * 100}%` }"
    />
  </template>
</div>
```
**Block structure:** 1px tall bar below the domain header, **before the nested PDT grid**. User wants it **removed**.

---

### Recommendation: Lightest-Touch Path

**The 3-bucket regrouping should be a presentation-only transform inside OverviewView**, not an API-layer concept:

1. **No DB/schema changes** — domains stay as-is (5 records)
2. **No API changes** — `LayerPayload` stays unchanged
3. **Client-side mapping** — add a pure function `superBucketFor(domainKey: string): "rnd" | "product" | "other"`:
   - `airnd` → `"rnd"`
   - `prodsw` → `"product"`
   - `hardware`, `proddigi`, `infra` → `"other"`
4. **OverviewView refactor** — wrap the domain cards in a super-bucket section with its own header/stats rollup

This avoids:
- Breaking the `Layer` binary toggle (still works for industry/public)
- Polluting the API surface (no new types, no new enum)
- Coupling the DB layer to presentation taxonomy (domains stay independent)

The 3 buckets become **visual grouping only**, resolvable inside the component via the mapping function, with no API overhead.
```

> AGENT

My honest take on the three asks:

1. **Compact tool row (status after name)** — agree. Current `ml-auto` right-aligns dots, which leaves awkward whitespace for short tool names. Tightening dots to sit immediately after the name (no push-to-right) will read as one cohesive line.

2. **3-bucket restructuring** — clean mapping. The seed has exactly 5 public domains: `airnd` ("AI R&D"), `prodsw` ("Product & Software"), `hardware`, `proddigi` ("Product Digitization"), `infra` ("Infrastructure"). So buckets 1 and 2 are 1:1 with existing domains; bucket 3 holds the remaining three. **No DB/API change needed** — this is a presentation-only re-bucketing inside `OverviewView`. Caveat: this re-bucketing only makes sense on the **public** layer (industry has 12 sectors that don't map to AI/Product/Other). So Overview-on-public will visually diverge from Panorama/List, which is defensible for an at-a-glance mode but worth flagging.

3. **Remove the status bar on domain card** — agree. The bar is largely redundant with the `% released` number already in the header. Removing reduces noise and saves vertical pixels (helpful for the single-viewport goal).

Two design choices need clarification before I write the plan:

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Overview view — 3-bucket sections, compact rows, drop redundant bar

## Context

PR #39 landed the Overview view with nested domain → PDT → tools containment. The user wants three follow-up refinements that together tighten the layout and reorganize the public-layer surface:

1. **Compact tool rows.** Today the status dots float to the right of the name via `ml-auto`, leaving awkward whitespace on short names. Drop the push so dots sit immediately after the tool name.
2. **3-bucket regrouping on the public layer.** The 5 existing domains (`airnd`, `prodsw`, `hardware`, `proddigi`, `infra`) map cleanly onto three named sections rendered top-to-bottom: **AI R&D**, **Product & Software**, **Hardware, Digitization & Infra**. The first two buckets each contain a single domain card; the third holds three domain cards in a horizontal sub-grid.
3. **Remove the domain-level status bar.** The thin segmented bar under the domain header duplicates the `% released` figure already in the same line — drop it to reduce visual noise and reclaim vertical space.

Industry layer stays unchanged: 12 flat sector cards (the AI/Product/Other buckets are public-domain concepts and don't map to industry sectors). This is a presentation-only change — no DB, API, or shared schema changes; the existing `LayerPayload` is untouched.

## Changes

### `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (single file edit)

**Script:**
- Add a `BUCKET_FOR: Record<string, "rnd" | "product" | "other">` constant mapping the 5 known public domain keys (`airnd → "rnd"`, `prodsw → "product"`, `hardware | proddigi | infra → "other"`). Unknown keys fall through to `"other"`.
- Add an `OverviewBucket` interface: `{ key: "rnd" | "product" | "other", title: string, items: DomainGroup[] }`.
- Add a `buckets` computed that, on the **public** layer, partitions the incoming domain groups into three `OverviewBucket`s in fixed order (`rnd`, `product`, `other`), filtering out empty buckets so a fully-filtered status query doesn't render bare section headers.
- Keep helpers: `dotClass`, `mcpTitle`, `pdtMcpCount`. No need for `statsFor` (already absent).

**Template — public layer (new):**
- Outer wrapper switches from a single auto-fit grid to a vertical `flex flex-col gap-5` of `<section>`s, one per bucket.
- Each bucket section gets a lightweight header: serif title (`text-[18px]`) + mono `(count)` of domains, no border-bottom — quiet inline header that doesn't compete with the inner domain-card headers.
- Bucket body is a grid: `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))` for the `other` bucket (so the 3 domain cards lay out 1–3 across based on viewport), and `1fr` single-column for the `rnd` and `product` buckets so the single domain card takes the full width.
- Each domain card renders the same existing inner structure (nested PDT sub-blocks → tool rows) **minus the status bar block** (see below).

**Template — industry layer (unchanged behavior, sector cards stay flat):**
- Iterate `props.groups` directly (no bucketing). One sector card per group, header + tool rows. **The status bar under the sector header is also removed** for consistency with the public layer.

**Domain / sector header — remove the bar:**
- Delete the `<div class="flex h-1 w-full rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-(--color-border) shrink-0">…</div>` block that sits between the header and the body. The `% released` number stays in the header line (already there); it carries the same information more compactly.

**Tool row — compact (status after name):**
- In both the sector tool row and the inner PDT-block tool row, change the dots container from `<div class="… ml-auto">` to `<div class="…">` (drop `ml-auto`), and tighten the gap between the name `<span>` and the dots container from `gap-1.5` → `gap-1.5` (sector) / `gap-1` (PDT) — already small, but the removal of `ml-auto` is the real change.
- Keep dot sizing: `size-[7px]` in sector rows, `size-[6px]` in PDT sub-block rows.
- Keep the `truncate shrink` on the name `<span>` so long names still clip rather than push dots off-row.

### `i18n/locales/en.json` and `zh.json` (3 new strings)

Add under the existing `mcpPanorama` namespace:

```jsonc
"bucket": {
  "rnd": "AI R&D",          // zh: "AI 研发"
  "product": "Product & Software",  // zh: "产品与软件"
  "other": "Hardware, Digitization & Infra"  // zh: "硬件、数字化与基础设施"
}
```

### No other files change

- `app/composables/usePanoramaState.ts` — unchanged.
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` — unchanged (the three-button toggle is correct).
- `app/pages/mcp/panorama.vue` — unchanged (still passes `filteredGroups` to `OverviewView`).
- `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts`, `server/utils/queries/mcp-landscape.ts`, `server/api/internal/mcp-landscape.get.ts` — unchanged. 3-bucket grouping is presentation-only.

## Verification

1. `bun run lint` — clean.
2. `bun run typecheck` — exit 0 (baseline tooling warnings only).
3. `bun run test` — all 256 existing tests still pass; no new tests required (purely visual refinement; the bucket-mapping function is a tiny pure lookup not worth a dedicated test).
4. **Manual browser check (user-side):** `bun run dev`, then:
   - `/en/mcp/panorama` (public, default = overview) — confirm three labeled sections stack top-to-bottom: AI R&D (one card), Product & Software (one card), Hardware, Digitization & Infra (three cards laid out 1–3 wide depending on viewport). Confirm the segmented status bar is gone from every domain card header.
   - `/en/mcp/panorama?layer=industry` — confirm flat sector cards (no bucketing), and confirm the status bar is gone there too.
   - `/en/mcp/panorama?status=released` (or `dev` / `none`) — confirm sections that lose all their domains after filtering vanish entirely (no orphan section header).
   - `/zh/mcp/panorama` — confirm the three Chinese bucket labels render correctly.
   - `?view=panorama` and `?view=list` — confirm those modes are untouched and the segmented bar inside `LayerSummary` (the regular panorama) remains intact.
   - Click a tool's dot → existing detail panel still opens.
   - Click a domain title → drill still works (URL gains `?primary=...`).

## Critical files

- `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` — modified (add bucketing on public, remove status bars on both layers, tighten tool rows)
- `i18n/locales/en.json` and `i18n/locales/zh.json` — add `mcpPanorama.bucket.{rnd,product,other}`
- Reference only (no edits): `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` (domain key truth), `shared/mcp-panorama.ts` (`Group` discriminator), `app/components/mcp-landscape/LayerSummary.vue` (regular panorama's status bar — must stay intact)

## Commit & branch flow

- Stay on the existing branch `feat/panorama-overview-mode` (the branch PR #39 is open against). Do **not** create a new branch.
- Commit as a coherent unit on top of `ae54b52` — a single `feat(mcp-panorama): …` commit covering the bucket sections, the dropped status bar, and the compact tool rows, since those three changes are tightly coupled visual refinements that ship together. No `Co-Authored-By:` trailer.
- **Do not push and do not open or update any PR.** The user will review the commit locally and push themselves when satisfied; once pushed, PR #39 will automatically pick up the new commit.

Committed as `e8c541a` on `feat/panorama-overview-mode`, sitting on top of `ae54b52`. Nothing pushed — your local branch is now 1 commit ahead of `origin/feat/panorama-overview-mode`.

## What landed in this commit

- **`app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue`** — Added a `BUCKET_FOR` map (`airnd → "rnd"`, `prodsw → "product"`, `hardware|proddigi|infra → "other"`, unknown → `"other"`) and a `buckets` computed that partitions public-layer domain groups into three fixed-order sections, dropping empty ones. The public-layer template now renders a vertical `flex flex-col gap-5` of bucket sections, each with a serif `text-[18px]` header + `(count)` of domains, and a per-bucket grid that goes `1fr` when there's only one domain (rnd, product) or `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))` for the "other" bucket. Industry layer keeps the flat sector grid unchanged structurally.
- **Status bar removed** on both layers — the `<div class="flex h-1 w-full rounded-full …">` block between the card header and body is gone. The `% released` figure in the header already conveys the same signal.
- **Tool rows tightened** — dropped `ml-auto justify-end` from the dot containers in both sector rows (`size-[7px]` dots) and PDT inner rows (`size-[6px]` dots), so dots now sit immediately after the tool name instead of being pushed to the right edge.
- **`i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json`** — added `mcpPanorama.bucket.{rnd,product,other}` in both locales.

## Verification

- `bun run lint` clean.
- `bun run typecheck` exit 0.
- `bun run test` — 256/256.

## Summary

**2. PDT grid in OverviewView.vue:**
- Grid: `grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));` (line 147)
- Gap: `gap-2` (line 146)
- Page wrapper: `px-7` (line 104) = 28px left+right padding
- Bucket section gaps: `gap-3` on domain card grid (line 117), `gap-2` on PDT grid container

At typical 1280px viewport: 1280 − 56px (padding) = 1224px available. With `gap-3` (12px) per bucket grid and 6 PDTs at 150px min each = 900px + gaps ≈ 1050px content, fits one line. Wrapping to two lines suggests viewport squeeze or gap rendering issue.

> AGENT

My honest take on both:

1. **Thinner PDT cards / AI R&D on one line** — agree. AI R&D has 6 PDTs. With current `minmax(150px, 1fr)` and a browse-layout sidebar eating ~280px on a 1280px viewport, the available width is ~944px and 6 × 150 = 900 + gaps spills marginally to 2 rows. Reducing the min PDT width (to ~110px) and tightening internal padding fits 6 on one line at typical widths while still leaving tools readable; the tool-name `truncate shrink` already handles squeeze. Same change benefits the "other" bucket cards too.

2. **Pill-shaped MCP indicators** — stylistic upgrade, fits the editorial theme well. Real tradeoff: a pill takes ~2× the horizontal width of a same-height dot, so a tool with 6+ MCPs will push the name to truncate more. I'll propose modest pill ratios (sector `w-[10px] h-[4px]`, PDT `w-[8px] h-[3px]`) — still definitively pill-shaped but barely wider than the current 7px / 6px dots so we don't trade legibility of names for the new shape.

No further clarification needed; pill dimensions are easy to eyeball after the first render. Writing the plan.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/the-blocks-still-need-fluffy-whistle.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Overview view — fit AI R&D on one line + pill-shaped MCP indicators\n\n## Context\n\nPR #39 (commits `ae54b52` and `e8c541a`) shipped the new Overview view with three super-bucket sections, nested PDT containment, and removed status bars. Two visual tweaks remain:\n\n1. **AI R&D currently wraps to two rows of PDT sub-blocks.** That domain has 6 PDTs (`sysdesign`, `devsvcs`, `testsvcs`, `rndmaint`, `aiprod`, `knowsvcs`); the current `minmax(150px, 1fr)` grid plus a browse-layout sidebar consuming ~280px on a typical 1280px viewport leaves only ~944px content — 6 × 150 + gaps overshoots. Tightening the PDT min-width (and trimming internal padding) lets all 6 PDTs sit on one row at common widths while keeping tool names readable via the existing `truncate shrink`.\n\n2. **MCP indicators are currently circular dots.** The user wants them pill-shaped (\"药丸\") — wider than tall with fully rounded ends — so MCPs read more like deployment artifacts and less like a status traffic light. Real tradeoff: pills are wider than dots, so tools with many MCPs push their names to truncate harder; we pick a *modest* pill ratio so the visual change is unambiguous without forcing names to disappear.\n\nThis is a pure visual tuning pass — no schema, no API, no logic change.\n\n## Changes\n\n### `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (single file edit)\n\n**PDT sub-block grid (public layer, domain card body):**\n- Inner grid: change `grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))` → `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(110px, 1fr))`. With 6 PDTs × 110px = 660px + 5 × 8px gap = 700px, AI R&D fits one row even with the sidebar.\n- PDT panel padding: tighten `px-2 py-1.5` → `px-1.5 py-1` so each panel takes less horizontal real estate without losing legibility.\n- Inner PDT header gap: change `gap-1.5` → `gap-1` (mono count was already small).\n\n**MCP indicators — pill shape:**\n- Sector tool row dot: replace `size-[7px] rounded-full` with `w-[10px] h-[4px] rounded-full` (2.5:1 pill, ~same vertical footprint).\n- PDT tool row dot: replace `size-[6px] rounded-full` with `w-[8px] h-[3px] rounded-full` (~2.7:1 pill).\n- Active-MCP ring (`ring-2 ring-(--color-ink)/40 ring-offset-1`) renders cleanly on pills — no change needed.\n- Hover scale (`hover:scale-[1.6]` / `hover:scale-[1.7]`) is proportional and works for pills.\n- Keep `opacity-40` placeholder treatment, status color via existing `dotClass`.\n\n**Side effect to watch:** PDT inner tool-row dot container currently uses `gap-[2px]`. Pills are taller-aspect-ratio so they read fine at 2px gap; no change needed. Sector tool-row stays at `gap-[3px]`.\n\n### No other files change\n\n- No new i18n strings (status labels already exist).\n- No script-side changes in OverviewView (no new computed, no new helpers).\n- No other component touched. The regular Panorama view (`PanoramaView.vue` → `McpTile.vue`) keeps its existing pill-style chip rendering and is unaffected.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun run lint` — clean.\n2. `bun run typecheck` — exit 0.\n3. `bun run test` — 256/256 must still pass (purely styling change).\n4. **Manual browser check (user-side):** `bun run dev`, then:\n   - `/en/mcp/panorama` (default = overview, public) — AI R&D bucket: all 6 PDTs (`sysdesign`, `devsvcs`, `testsvcs`, `rndmaint`, `aiprod`, `knowsvcs`) should sit in one row at viewport widths ≥ ~1200px. At narrower widths the grid will wrap naturally.\n   - Confirm MCP indicators are now pills (wider than tall) in both sector and PDT contexts, with the same status colors.\n   - `/en/mcp/panorama?layer=industry` — sector tool rows show the wider pill style.\n   - `?view=panorama` and `?view=list` — confirm those modes still use the existing detailed pill-chip rendering from `McpTile.vue` and are untouched.\n\n## Critical files\n\n- `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` — modified (PDT grid min-width + padding + MCP pill shape in two tool-row contexts)\n- Reference only (no edits): `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` (confirms AI R&D has 6 PDTs), `app/components/mcp-landscape/McpTile.vue` (existing pill chip rendering — design reference)\n\n## Commit & branch flow\n\n- Stay on the existing branch `feat/panorama-overview-mode`. Commit as a single `style(mcp-panorama): …` commit on top of `e8c541a`, since both tweaks are visual-only and ship together. ## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Overview view — fit AI R&D on one line + pill-shaped MCP indicators

## Context

PR #39 (commits `ae54b52` and `e8c541a`) shipped the new Overview view with three super-bucket sections, nested PDT containment, and removed status bars. Two visual tweaks remain:

1. **AI R&D currently wraps to two rows of PDT sub-blocks.** That domain has 6 PDTs (`sysdesign`, `devsvcs`, `testsvcs`, `rndmaint`, `aiprod`, `knowsvcs`); the current `minmax(150px, 1fr)` grid plus a browse-layout sidebar consuming ~280px on a typical 1280px viewport leaves only ~944px content — 6 × 150 + gaps overshoots. Tightening the PDT min-width (and trimming internal padding) lets all 6 PDTs sit on one row at common widths while keeping tool names readable via the existing `truncate shrink`.

2. **MCP indicators are currently circular dots.** The user wants them pill-shaped ("药丸") — wider than tall with fully rounded ends — so MCPs read more like deployment artifacts and less like a status traffic light. Real tradeoff: pills are wider than dots, so tools with many MCPs push their names to truncate harder; we pick a *modest* pill ratio so the visual change is unambiguous without forcing names to disappear.

This is a pure visual tuning pass — no schema, no API, no logic change.

## Changes

### `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (single file edit)

**PDT sub-block grid (public layer, domain card body):**
- Inner grid: change `grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr))` → `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(110px, 1fr))`. With 6 PDTs × 110px = 660px + 5 × 8px gap = 700px, AI R&D fits one row even with the sidebar.
- PDT panel padding: tighten `px-2 py-1.5` → `px-1.5 py-1` so each panel takes less horizontal real estate without losing legibility.
- Inner PDT header gap: change `gap-1.5` → `gap-1` (mono count was already small).

**MCP indicators — pill shape:**
- Sector tool row dot: replace `size-[7px] rounded-full` with `w-[10px] h-[4px] rounded-full` (2.5:1 pill, ~same vertical footprint).
- PDT tool row dot: replace `size-[6px] rounded-full` with `w-[8px] h-[3px] rounded-full` (~2.7:1 pill).
- Active-MCP ring (`ring-2 ring-(--color-ink)/40 ring-offset-1`) renders cleanly on pills — no change needed.
- Hover scale (`hover:scale-[1.6]` / `hover:scale-[1.7]`) is proportional and works for pills.
- Keep `opacity-40` placeholder treatment, status color via existing `dotClass`.

**Side effect to watch:** PDT inner tool-row dot container currently uses `gap-[2px]`. Pills are taller-aspect-ratio so they read fine at 2px gap; no change needed. Sector tool-row stays at `gap-[3px]`.

### No other files change

- No new i18n strings (status labels already exist).
- No script-side changes in OverviewView (no new computed, no new helpers).
- No other component touched. The regular Panorama view (`PanoramaView.vue` → `McpTile.vue`) keeps its existing pill-style chip rendering and is unaffected.

## Verification

1. `bun run lint` — clean.
2. `bun run typecheck` — exit 0.
3. `bun run test` — 256/256 must still pass (purely styling change).
4. **Manual browser check (user-side):** `bun run dev`, then:
   - `/en/mcp/panorama` (default = overview, public) — AI R&D bucket: all 6 PDTs (`sysdesign`, `devsvcs`, `testsvcs`, `rndmaint`, `aiprod`, `knowsvcs`) should sit in one row at viewport widths ≥ ~1200px. At narrower widths the grid will wrap naturally.
   - Confirm MCP indicators are now pills (wider than tall) in both sector and PDT contexts, with the same status colors.
   - `/en/mcp/panorama?layer=industry` — sector tool rows show the wider pill style.
   - `?view=panorama` and `?view=list` — confirm those modes still use the existing detailed pill-chip rendering from `McpTile.vue` and are untouched.

## Critical files

- `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` — modified (PDT grid min-width + padding + MCP pill shape in two tool-row contexts)
- Reference only (no edits): `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` (confirms AI R&D has 6 PDTs), `app/components/mcp-landscape/McpTile.vue` (existing pill chip rendering — design reference)

## Commit & branch flow

- Stay on the existing branch `feat/panorama-overview-mode`. Commit as a single `style(mcp-panorama): …` commit on top of `e8c541a`, since both tweaks are visual-only and ship together. No `Co-Authored-By:` trailer.
- Do not push automatically — wait for explicit "push it" from the user, same as last round.

  - PDT inner tool row: `size-[6px] rounded-full` → `w-[8px] h-[3px] rounded-full`

Active ring, hover scale, status color, and placeholder opacity all carry over unchanged on the new pill geometry.

1	# Plan: Overview view — fit AI R&D on one line + pill-shaped MCP indicators
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	PR #39 (commits `ae54b52` and `e8c541a`) shipped the new Overview view with three super-bucket sections, nested PDT containment, and removed status bars. Two visual tweaks remain:
6	
7	1. **AI R&D currently wraps to two rows of PDT sub-blocks.** That domain has 6 PDTs (`sysdesign`, `devsvcs`, `testsvcs`, `rndmaint`, `aiprod`, `knowsvcs`); the current `minmax(150px, 1fr)` grid plus a browse-layout sidebar consuming ~280px on a typical 1280px viewport leaves only ~944px content — 6 × 150 + gaps overshoots. Tightening the PDT min-width (and trimming internal padding) lets all 6 PDTs sit on one row at common widths while keeping tool names readable via the existing `truncate shrink`.
8	
9	2. **MCP indicators are currently circular dots.** The user wants them pill-shaped ("药丸") — wider than tall with fully rounded ends — so MCPs read more like deployment artifacts and less like a status traffic light. Real tradeoff: pills are wider than dots, so tools with many MCPs push their names to truncate harder; we pick a *modest* pill ratio so the visual change is unambiguous without forcing names to disappear.
10	
11	This is a pure visual tuning pass — no schema, no API, no logic change.
12	
13	## Changes

15	### `app/components/mcp-landscape/OverviewView.vue` (single file edit)

Perfect. Now I have all the information I need. Let me write a clear report (under 400 words as requested):

---

## MCP Landscape Seed Pipeline Map

**1. Exported Structure from `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts`:**

- **`McpStatus`** = `"released" | "dev" | "none"`
- **`INDUSTRY_SECTORS: McpSectorSeed[]`** — 12 sectors (wireless, datacom, cloud, etc.)
  - Fields: `key` (required, PK), `label`, `labelZh`, `short`
- **`PUBLIC_DOMAINS: McpDomainSeed[]`** — 5 domains (AI R&D, Hardware, etc.) + nested PDTs
  - Fields: `key`, `label`, `labelZh`, `short`, `pdts[]`
  - PDTs: `key`, `label`, `labelZh`
- **`MCP_TOOLS: McpToolSeed[]`** — the product headers (tool owns 0–N MCPs)
  - Fields: `slug` (required), `name`, `nameZh`, `owner` (required, e.g., `"wireless"` or `"airnd.sysdesign"`), `blurb`, `blurbZh`, `mcps[]`
- **`McpSeed`** — individual MCP
  - Fields: `slug` (required, PK), `name?`, `nameZh?`, `released` (boolean), `inDev` (boolean), `depsCount`, `blurb`, `blurbZh`, `tags: string[]`

**In-dev MCPs** are represented via `inDev: true` + `released: false`. **Tools with zero MCPs** use `mcps: []` (the `noMcpTool()` helper); at runtime they render a single greyed-out placeholder tile. **Placeholder MCPs** don't exist in the seed — the frontend generates them client-side.

**2. Seed Behavior (`scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts`):**

Idempotent, non-destructive **upsert** — never truncates. Order:
1. Upsert `default` organization (system owner for extension stubs)
2. Upsert sectors by `key`
3. Upsert domains by `key`
4. Upsert PDTs by composite `key` (e.g., `airnd.sysdesign`)
5. Create marketplace extension stubs for all `released` MCPs (id: `mcp-{slug}`)
6. Upsert tool rows (slug is the surrogate key)
7. Upsert MCP rows (slug is the surrogate key, FK to tool via `toolId`)
8. **Prune stale MCPs/extensions** that are no longer in the canonical `MCP_TOOLS` inventory

Does NOT mutate sectors when only public data changes.

**3. Schema (`shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts`):**

| Table | NOT NULL columns |
|-------|-----------------|
| `mcp_landscape_tools` | `slug`, `name`, `layer`, `blurb`, `blurbZh` |
| `mcp_landscape_mcps` | `toolId`, `slug`, `name`, `blurb`, `blurbZh`, `inDev` |

**Status location**: `mcp_landscape_mcps.extensionId` (non-null → released), `inDev` (in-dev flag). **Layer column**: `mcp_landscape_tools.layer` enum (`"industry"` \| `"public"`).

FKs: `ownerSector` → `mcpSectors.key`, `ownerDomain` → `mcpDomains.key`, `ownerPdt` → `mcpPdts.key`.

**4. Parsing helpers:**

None exist. The seed reads `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` directly. You'll write a one-off text-tree parser.

**5. Re-run command:**

```bash
bun run db:seed:mcp
```

Requires `DATABASE_URL` env var. Idempotent; safe to re-run on deploy (wired into `vercel-build`). No prerequisite cleanup — migrations must have run first.

---

**For your text-tree import**: Provide defaults for missing fields — slugs from names, English from Chinese if omitted, tags as empty array, depsCount as 0. The schema enforces no NULLs on core fields, so every tool/MCP row must carry `name`, `blurb`, `blurbZh`, and `inDev` at minimum.
```
